BEFORE
HONOURABLE THE CHIEF JUSTICE
HONOURABLE MR. JUSTICE SOUMITRA SAIKIA
ORDER
Date : 07-01-2022
Heard Mr. A. R. Bhuyan, learned counsel for the petitioners. Also heard
Mr. N. J. Khataniar, learned Standing Counsel, Elementary Education
Department, Assam, appearing for the State respondents as well as Mr. S.
Bora, learned counsel appearing for respondent no. 6.
In this writ petition, the case of the petitioners is that they are Central
Teachers Eligibility Test (TET) qualified candidates and are eligible for appointment
in primary schools. By this writ petition the petitioners are seeking quashing of the
advertisement dated 24.09.2020, by which they have been debarred from
participating in the selection process for appointment of teachers in the lower and
upper primary schools in the State of Assam as the advertisement specifically
states that only those candidates who are qualified in the TET conducted by the
Elementary Education Department, Government of Assam, shall be eligible to
apply.
In this case, the only question before us for determination is whether the
petitioners, who have passed the CTET, are qualified to be appointed as

Page No.# 282/282
Elementary Schools teachers or not.
Learned counsel for the petitioners has submitted that this case is covered by a
judgment dated 03.02.2021 passed by this Court in WA No. 24/2021, wherein this
Court held that the condition put in the aforesaid case that only the candidates
qualified in the TET conducted by the State of Assam were eligible to participate in
the selection process was violative of Article 14 of the Constitution of India and
considering the facts and circumstances of the case as also keeping in view the
legitimate expectation of the CTET qualified candidates, they were allowed to
participate in the selected process conducted by the State Government of Assam.
Learned Standing Counsel, Elementary Education Department, Assam, has also
fairly submitted that this case is squarely covered by the judgment passed by this
Court in WA No. 24/2021.
In view of the above, this writ petition is disposed of in terms of the judgment
dated 03.02.2021 passed by this Court in WA No. 24/2021.
However, since most of the appointments have already taken place in the year
2020, we make it very clear that the appointment of the present petitioners shall
be made subject to availability of vacancies for that year.
JUDGE CHIEF JUSTICE
Comparing Assistant
